Since the beginning of our term of office, we have been trying to remove glass ceilings. We are trying to remove barriers for these groups of society, for those broad masses of society, which in previous years, in the quarter of the century of the Third Republic, had by far the most difficult. Of course, such groups include families of many children, hence our great 500+ program, since July 1 500+ for the first child as well. These groups were seniors, retired people all the time. Such groups are, of course, persons with disabilities. This is a special group of people who, as a result of an unfortunate fate or even from birth or from some accident, are deprived of the possibility of independent existence. It is a group that, on the part of our society as a whole, requires special care, special care and support. That is why we have decided to carry out another programme - I do not hesitate to call it simply a social programme - which will try to help at least a little bit in life those who are most affected by fate, people with disabilities. Democracy must also be a democracy with a conscience. It cannot be a democracy that takes little account of the needs of people with disabilities. This is the measure of humanity. That is why we will also try in the coming years, if voters decide in this way in the autumn, a super-proportionary fund for social spending will be spent on helping people with disabilities. ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u5	Thank you, Prime Minister. At the moment, I ask the Secretary of State at the Ministry of Family, Labour and Social Policy of Mr Krzysztof Michałkiewicz to speak.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u6	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Prime Minister! I would like to thank everyone and, in particular, the Prime Minister for the initiative that concerns the people most in need of our support and assistance. I wanted to say hello to all those unable to exist alone who are watching, following the transmission from the Sejm's deliberations. This bill is for you. The proposed law aims to provide additional means to meet the needs of people unable to live on their own because they bear increased costs of working in everyday and social life, care, rehabilitation and medical care. A supplementary benefit of PLN 500 per month may be claimed by persons who are over 18 years of age and whose inability to self-existence has been determined by a judgment on total incapacity for work and self-existence, or by a decision on incapacity to self-existence, or by a decision on total incapacity to work on a farm and self-existence, or by a judgment on total incapacity to serve and self-existence. As you can see, we take into account the various decisions of different insurance institutions, so that any person who is unable to live on his own can benefit from this support. The proposed benefit shall be paid to persons who do not have the right to cash benefits financed by public funds or whose benefit does not exceed the amount of the lowest pension for total incapacity for work. This is very important because there are many misunderstandings, misunderstandings in the public debate, in the media. There is no income criterion here, please, but we want the benefit to go to all those who either do not receive any public benefits or receive benefits that do not exceed the amount of the lowest disability pension. I would like to remind you that the lowest pension for incapacity for work is valued every year. On 1 March, it was wiped out and we assume it will also increase in the following year. It should be noted that the right to supplementary benefits will not be affected by receiving the care allowance and other allowances and benefits paid together with the benefit so far. It must also be strongly emphasized that we exclude from this all kinds of additions: a care supplement, but also a mattress supplement and various kinds of additions that are obtained when witnessing, e.g. an energy depot or an apartment supplement. All such additional benefits, which are linked to the basic benefit, will not affect the receipt of the supplementary benefit. The right to benefit will be established on request - in order to obtain the benefit, the person concerned will have to submit an application - to the person entitled to pay pension benefits and, in the case of persons who do not have the right to cash benefits financed by public funds or who are entitled to social assistance benefits, to the Social Insurance Institution. Once again, you must apply to receive the benefit. The application is submitted to this body, which pays us pensions or pensions. If we do not have the right to any benefits or have the right only to social assistance, we apply to the Social Insurance Institution. Persons having on the date of entry into force of the Act a decision on a significant degree of disability issued by district or voivodeship court teams will be able to apply to the Social Insurance Institution for a decision on incapacity for independent existence within 6 months. Where such a decision is given, the supplementary benefit will be granted from the month of submission of the application. After that period, the right to supplementary benefits will be determined on the basis of the rules laid down in the proposed law, whereas it is noted that the period will not be longer than 30 days from the date of clarification of the last circumstances necessary for the adoption of the decision. We also propose to improve the ruling that is being conducted by the Social Insurance Institution, so that not only doctors, but also other doctors, members of the medical committees, can issue judgments. Of course, there will also be the possibility that people who, due to their independence, cannot leave their place of residence will be reached by the ruling team and judgment will take place at their place of residence. So this is also envisaged as an facilitation for these people. The point is that anyone who needs a judgment of independence gets a ruling. In addition, in order to improve the process of applying for supplementary benefits and reduce the costs involved, it is proposed to amend the provisions of the Social Rent Act in order to determine the total incapacity for work, the juror, with the consent of the applicant, notified no later than during the examination of that person, to determine at the same time the inability of that person to self-exist. As you know, disabled persons, from birth disabled persons or those for whom disability was created before they were old age, may receive a social pension, but in order to receive this social pension, they must have a judgment from the Social Insurance Institution on total incapacity for work. Now, as the total incapacity of these people to work will be determined, it will also be possible to say that they are unable to exist on their own, and later this ruling will be sufficient to seek the benefit of 500+. An important solution included in the submitted project is to guarantee the person entitled to pay the full amount of the benefit. To this end, it is proposed that the supplementary benefit be exempted from deductions and executions. Both the benefit and the costs of handling the award process will be financed by the Solidarity Fund for the Support of Persons with Disabilities. In order to enable the implementation of the Act without interference and to grant the benefit to persons unable to self-exist, the project provides for the possibility of granting a loan from the Labour Fund to the Solidarity Fund for the Support of Persons with Disabilities, including the procedure for granting it and the required procedure for the financial plans of the individual Funds. Here it is worth to add that, of course, the benefit of 500+ for people unable to self-exist will be tax-free. It will be exempt from taxes. Also in the case of people who, for example, they benefit from rehabilitation relief, this benefit will not be included. This benefit will be untaxed, will be received in full. At the same time, it will not affect, where the income criterion is required, to receive specific support. The project provides for the entry into force of the Regulation as from 1 October 2019, except for the provisions on special grants for the Solidarity Fund for the Support of Persons with Disabilities which enter into force on 1 January 2021. Very much for us: the Prime Minister, everyone in our ministry, and everyone in the Social Insurance Institution, is concerned that those who need such support receive this benefit as soon as possible. Hence this quick procedure and also a request to the state to approve this bill As soon as possible. The point is that the Social Insurance Institution will actually start working on preparing to accept applications and to pay the benefit this year. We want it to be in the last quarter of this year and that this benefit goes to people who are often without any means of subsistence or have these means of subsistence very small, incomparable to the costs associated with inability to exist independently, the costs associated with rehabilitation, medical care, care. These people are waiting for this, and they deserve it. Thank you very much. [[Applause]] [[Chairman of the session includes Deputy Marshal Ryszard Terlecki of the Sejm]]

Services for care and assistance for disabled people are being developed by increasing annual funding for environmental self-help homes, WTCs and ZAZs. Professional activation of disabled persons is carried out through projects and programmes implemented by PFRON. The amount of benefits and benefits addressed to disabled persons and their carers is systematically increased. Thus, since 1 September 2018, the Act increasing the social pension has entered into force, aligning it with the lowest pension due to total inability to work. [[Applause]] Thank you very much to the Government of Law and Justice, the Minister, the other ministries for all their activities for the benefit of people with disabilities. We proceed with the examination of item 2 of the agenda: First reading of the government draft law amending the Act on VAT on goods and services and some other laws (Print No 3602). I ask the Undersecretary of State at the Ministry of Finance of Mr Tadeusz Kościński to present the justification for the bill.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u106	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The main objective of the draft law is to introduce an obligation to use a split payment mechanism in sectors where many irregularities have been diagnosed in previous years. The project therefore aims to further seal the VAT system. The mandatory application of the split payment mechanism will apply to payments for goods or services which are currently covered by the reverse charge regime and tax liability. These include in particular fuel, steel and steel products, scrap and waste, precious metals, e.g. Gold and silver, and baseless, e.g. copper, stretch film, tablets, smartphones, consoles, construction services. In addition, this obligation will apply to payments for parts and accessories of motor vehicles, coal and coal products, electrical machinery and equipment and their parts and accessories. The detailed list of goods and services covered by the clearing obligation using this mechanism shall include an annex to the Act. The compulsory form of the split payment mechanism will be covered by invoices with a gross value exceeding 15 000. zł and the invoice shall document the transaction in respect of the goods or services listed in the attached annex. Invoices of less than 15 thousand. PLN will be cleared on a general basis. In order to protect against fraud, transactions below 15 000 zł, the object of which is all goods listed in the Annex to the Act, are subject to tax liability. This responsibility will be applied from the first zloty. This draft provides for a special indication of the invoices that will be subject to compulsory settlement in the split payment. These invoices will have to include an endorsement of the split payment mechanism. If the taxable person fails to comply with the obligation to include that note, then the sanction shall apply. This penalty will amount to 30% of the VAT amount of the transaction covered by the clearing obligation using the split payment mechanism. However, this penalty will not be applied if, despite the lack of information on the invoice, the purchaser makes the payment in the correct form, i.e. using the split payment mechanism. A sanction has also been imposed on the purchaser who, despite the obligation to apply the split payment, will otherwise regulate the invoices. This penalty will also amount to 30% of the amount of VAT charged on the transaction subject to the clearing obligation using a split payment. This penalty will not be applied if the seller clears the entire tax from such an invoice. The proposal also contains important facilitations for taxpayers in the application of the split payment mechanism, which will cover both the voluntary form that has been in operation since the previous year and the mandatory form introduced by this project. These facilitations are: extending the possibility of regulating from the VAT account also other fiscal charges, such as VAT on imports, income taxes, excise duties, customs duties and contributions to the Social Security Office, which will virtually eliminate the liquidity problem, as it will allow the use of VAT funds from the VAT account smoothly almost throughout the month; introducing the possibility of making aggregate transfers under the split payment mechanism, introducing the possibility of adjusting advances before receipt of invoices, allowing for reciprocal deductions and compensation. A penalty shall also be imposed on the purchaser in PIT and CIT taxes in the form of a loss of the right to credit expenditure to the cost of obtaining revenue in the event of payment other than the split payment mechanism. However, this penalty will be limited to cases where the invoice has been correctly marked and the buyer will nevertheless pay in a different form. The draft also provides for the addition of a new criminal act in the Criminal Code sanctioning the failure of taxpayers to pay by means of a split payment mechanism, imposing a fine of 720 daily rates and, in the case of a minor measure, a fine as for a tax offence. Another change in the project is the exemption from the tax charge of applications for the release of funds from the VAT account. It is assumed that the proposed law amending the Law on value added tax and certain other laws will enter into force on 1 September this year, except for the provisions on income taxes which will enter into force later on 1 January 2020. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Thanks to the proposed regulation, we expect the following effects, firstly, to seal the system and, as a result, to reduce the VAT gap, secondly, to protect the state budget against the risk of VAT not being discharged by the supplier and, thirdly, to improve the business conditions by increasing the certainty of economic turnover, to reduce the unauthorised advantage that tax fraud operators have had so far over honest VAT taxpayers. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The proposed regulations will have an impact on the increase in the revenue of the state budget and a minimal reduction in the revenue of local government units. The changes will have a positive financial impact on the revenue of the State budget from VAT. In the first year of the regulation, these effects will amount to about PLN 240 million and in the following years to PLN 960 million. The proposed changes will have a negative financial impact on the revenue of local government units due to the tax charge, which will amount to around 300,000. PLN per year. In view of the above, I kindly ask the High House to refer this proposal to further parliamentary work. Thank you for your attention. [[Applause]] [[Chairman of the proceedings includes Deputy Marshal Stanisław Tyszka of the Sejm]]
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u107	Thank you, Minister. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. Mr Andrzej Noblet, Law and Justice have the floor.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u108	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! I have the honour to present the position of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice on the Government's draft law amending the law on value added tax and certain other laws, print No 3602. The purpose of the amendment to the VAT Act is to replace the current special settlement of VAT, i.e. the reverse charge, with a compulsory split payment mechanism. The draft law also introduces changes to the rules on tax liability. The main amendments contained in this draft law concern the Act of 11 March 2004 on the tax on goods and services and the Act of 29 August 1997. The compulsory form of the split payment mechanism is to be applied to the supply of goods and services which are currently subject to the reverse charge procedure and the existing scope of tax liability. In addition, the draft law proposes to include a compulsory split payment mechanism for those transactions which are the subject of components and accessories for motor vehicles, coal and coal products, electrical machinery and equipment, their parts and accessories and other goods and services listed in Annex 15 thereto. The adoption of the amendments proposed in this project will result in the elimination of the principle of the reverse charge applied in Poland so far. The split payment mechanism will apply to goods and services documented by invoices whose gross value exceeds 15 000. PLN or equivalent. This amount has been correlated with the amount above which the payment by bank transfer is subject to payment. For transactions below 15 000 zł The purchaser may also use a split payment mechanism on a voluntary basis. A comprehensive analysis of the effectiveness of this solution was an inspiration to replace the reverse charge with a split payment mechanism. The split payment mechanism has been in operation in Poland since 2018. It is an innovative and unique solution on an international scale. Innovation is based on this mechanism with the banking system, which means that the funds collected in the VAT account remain the property of the taxable person and can benefit from these funds. Only the objectives for which these funds may be earmarked are limited. This mechanism prevents VAT fraud by so-called. disappearing taxpayers and at the same time provides protection for the buyer of goods or services, guaranteeing him the tax security of transactions. It provides legal protection against the possible use of it by an unfair counterparty. The basis for the proposed amendments is Decision 2019/310 of the Council of the European Union of 18 February this year authorising Poland to introduce a specific measure derogating from Article. 226 Directive 2006/112/EC on the common system of value added tax. With regard to tax liability, the tax services have identified the use of a safeguard mechanism, i.e. a guarantee deposit, which is why the bill proposes to terminate the guarantee deposit and to amend the rules on the application of tax liability. According to the applicants, the introduction of a split payment mechanism will have a positive impact on business conditions by increasing trading certainty [[♪ The bell ♪]] the economic and competitive position of honest entrepreneurs. Therefore, the Law and Justice Club will support this project in the further legislative procedure. Thank you very much.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u109	Thank you. Mr Włodzimierz Nykiel, Civic Platform - Civic Coalition.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u110	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Civic Platform - Civic Coalition Parliamentary Club, I have the honour to present an opinion on the draft law amending the act on value added tax and certain other laws, print No 3602. The draft provides for the introduction of provisions in the Goods and Services Tax Act requiring the application of a split payment mechanism in certain sectors of the economy susceptible to fraud after exceeding the quota threshold in the gross invoice value. The mandatory split payment mechanism will generally cover the supply of goods and services currently covered by the reverse charge and joint and several liability listed in Annexes 11, 13 and 14 to the Act, and in addition the supply of parts and accessories for motor vehicles, coal and coal products, electrical machinery and equipment and their parts and accessories, as well as electrical equipment and their parts and accessories. Goods and services covered by the compulsory split payment mechanism are listed in the new Annex 15. In simplification, the aim of the project is primarily to replace the reverse charge mechanism in national transactions with a compulsory split payment mechanism. The project provides for the quota threshold for the compulsory application of the split payment mechanism. This obligation will be incurred in the case of payment for invoices with a gross value exceeding 15 000. zł. The draft also proposes amendments to the Tax Criminal Code to introduce tax liability for the taxpayer who made payments without the mandatory split payment mechanism. The taxable person who issued the invoice without stating the split payment will bear the penalty tax liability for issuing the invoice in a faulty manner. What comments can be made about this project? It contains a provision repealing the obligation to submit summary information on national trade - Article. Point 1 (5) of the project in question. Interestingly, the provision providing for the abolition of the obligation to submit summary information is already contained in the Act of 4 July 2019 amending the Act on VAT on goods and services and certain other laws which await the signature of the President. Then why put up with this regulation twice? There are doubts about how to set a threshold for the mandatory split payment mechanism. The proposed provisions shall include: When payment is made for purchased goods or services documented by an invoice in which the total amount of the claim is the amount referred to in Article. 19 point 2 of the Business Law Act, taxpayers are obliged to apply a split payment mechanism. Art. 19 point 2 of the Business Law provides that: the making or acceptance of payments relating to the economic activity carried out shall be effected through the trader's payment account whenever the one-off value of the transaction exceeds 15 000, regardless of the number of payments resulting from it. zł. So we have to ask whether this is more than 15,000. PLN or by an amount equal to 15 thousand. zł, because in practice this is not the case, and after all there are penalties, there are sanctions. That should be perfectly clear. Ladies and gentlemen, we will support this project. A number of things have been reported by the Chairman of the Nobles. He said, among other things, The role of a bank camera. I think the banker is the beneficiary of this bill, but you will tell Minister Cichoń about it. Thank you. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u111	Thank you, sir. Thank you very much. Mr Paweł Grabowski, Kukiz Poselski Club15.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u112	Thank you. Mr. Marshal! Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u113	Thank you very much, sir. And I invite Mrs Genowefa Tokarska, Polish People's Party - Coalition Poland.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u114	Thank you. Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Polish People's Party - Coalition Poland I have the honour to present an opinion on the draft law on amending the act on tax on goods and services and on certain other laws, print No 3602. The draft law proposes replacing the current reverse charge mechanism with a mandatory, compulsory split payment mechanism. The split payment mechanism will cover all goods and services currently covered by the reverse charge mechanism, including inter alia. just scrap, steel, waste, electronics, fuel and will be extended to include transactions involving parts and accessories of motor vehicles, coal and coal products, electrical machinery and equipment and their parts and accessories. The split payment mechanism will cover goods or services documented by invoices whose gross value exceeds 15 000. PLN or equivalent. An amount of 15,000. The zloty is not random. It follows from the Law of Entrepreneurs - it is continuity - and the obligation to make payments above this amount without cash. These payments shall be made by bank transfer. This quota limit can be changed automatically, mutatis mutandis, in accordance with the Act of 6 March 2018. Below the amount of 15 thousand. zł The application of the split payment mechanism can be carried out on a voluntary basis, just as the split payment principle has been in operation so far, i.e. since 1 July 2018. The voluntary application of the split payment tool can also be applied in respect of other goods and services not necessarily covered by this project, this mandatory mechanism. The reverse VAT was not used for all goods and was extended by some elements only. The bill also provides for the liquidation of the guarantee deposit and amends the rules on the application of tax liability. When assessing the bill, it should be noted that the split payment mechanism will in our view certainly counter fraud and tax fraud more effectively than the reverse charge of VAT. The system will prevent fraud already at the stage of the transaction itself, i.e. in the initial stage, and will ensure better transparency of VAT settlements. It should also be stressed that this mechanism imposes huge obligations on the banking sector and, above all, will be a huge burden on businesses, particularly small and medium-sized enterprises, whose freezing of substantial financial resources will impede the liquidity of the accounts and reduce their activities. A dangerous element of the project is also the proposed date of entry into force of the essential regulations, as the Act provides that they will enter into force from 1 September 2019. The project thus breaks the good principle of tax regulation, which is used in good practice in the past from 1 January and 1 July. The assumed deadline, far too short, will give rise to some difficulties in adapting the company's online accounting system, which it includes. My club supports this bill. I think that there will be a substantive discussion on it and doubts will be clarified during the work of the committee. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u134	Thank you. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, It is my great honor to present to you a draft law amending the Personal Income Tax Act. I immediately warn you that this is about income tax in reporting to seafarers, whose proposal concerns very small but very important substantively. The aim of this project is to implement the position of the European Commission presented in the course of the procedure on notification of the aid measure to ship owners adopted in the Maritime Labour Act of August 2015, which amended the relevant provisions of the 1991 Act on Individual Income Tax. This aid measure consisted in exempting from income tax the income of seafarers obtained from working on ships of Polish nationality together for a period exceeding 183 days in the tax year in international shipping on ships used for the carriage of cargo or passengers. Ladies and gentlemen! This aid measure has been challenged by the European Commission and has not entered into force in the absence of a positive decision. In the opinion of the European Commission, this provision should be extended to maritime vessels belonging to all Member States of the European Union and the European Economic Area in order to prevent discrimination against those States. This solution is also applied in other Member States. Consequently, our draft law implies a change of article. Article 21 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 1 point 23c of the Personal Income Tax Act, which extends its scope to ships belonging to the European Union and the EEA. This will allow sailors working on ships not only under the Polish flag but also under the flags of the European Union and the EEA to obtain exemption from income tax. The proposed amendment will allow for a positive decision on the compatibility of State aid with the internal market by the European Commission, which has so far blocked us and thus this change, and the inclusion of Poland in the majority of Member States applying the Community guidelines on State aid to maritime transport, which recommend the application of aid measures in the field of labour costs and ship owners' taxes. This bill was prepared by the Tripartite Team acting at the Ministry of Maritime Affairs and Inland Navigation. Sailing and Sea Fishing and has full support from the public. Last week in our ministry there was a meeting of a trilateral team, all participants hoped that this amendment would be possible during this term of office. At the request of the social side, the project shall also indicate periods equivalent to periods at sea during which a seafarer may not be present on board by his own fault, for example. the duration of the trip to and from the ship, or the time spent on compulsory training required to maintain or improve professional qualifications required by international law. This solution was initially accepted by the European Commission as being in line with the guidelines. Ladies and gentlemen! The overarching objective of the proposed law is to encourage shipowners to flag their ships off the flag, which guarantees seafarers tax exemptions, i.e. the cheap flag of countries such as Bahamas, Liberia, Panama, under the Polish flag, and to improve the competitiveness of Polish seafarers on the international maritime labour market, which, as you know, is flooded by seafarers from third countries. This follows the Act of 24 August 2006 on tonnage tax as a step towards Poland's implementation of the European Commission's recommendations. This is the first of three, i.e. The second of the three steps that the Government of Law and Justice has set itself the goal of completing, closing the legislative, tax instruments so that it can be returned to the Polish flag with a clear conscience and full pockets. The first step was taken in 2006 during the first rule of law and justice. Sleep. President Lech Kaczyński then signed the Tonnage Tax Act. She was introducing this tax for the ship owners. The second step is today, which is the Income Tax Act. The third step, ladies and gentlemen, is a law on the social security of seafarers at the President's Office. And then this whole three-pack will work, we hope, according to our calculations, but also according to the assessments of employers, shipowners and the social, trade union side, in favour of this overriding purpose, the posting and return of ships under the Polish flag. Thank you very much and I would like to ask for this amendment to be adopted. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u160	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Ladies and gentlemen! As I said, presenting this March the position of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice towards the government project, of course we agree with the objective which, as we read in the explanatory memorandum, was guiding the Council of Ministers in preparing this project. This is to improve criminal proceedings, to eliminate the perceived shortcomings and legislative errors that occur in the procedural law and to adapt procedural regulations to the challenges arising from technical progress. In particular, it is about abandoning unnecessary procedural formalism, making it easier for participants in proceedings related to participation in it, preventing the obstruction of the parties and protecting the social interest more widely in criminal proceedings. In Poland, ladies and gentlemen, there is a cross-party agreement that judicial proceedings should be speeded up. This also applies, to a particular extent, to criminal proceedings. Such simplification has long been advocated by Law and Justice. Similarly, we welcome the senate initiatives, one of which is the implementation of the request from the citizens' petition, and the other is the proposal to execute the judgment of the Constitutional Tribunal. Thanks to this, first of all, we put in the Code of Criminal Procedure a solution, according to which, if the end of the period for the execution of the action falls on a legal day declared free of work or on Saturday, the action may be carried out on the next day, which is not a day free of work or a Saturday. Its adoption will lead to uniform arrangements for the calculation of time limits for the execution of procedural acts in criminal, civil, administrative and tax proceedings. Second, there is a redrafting of § 56 of the Code of Criminal Procedure, which will allow defendants who have been deprived of the status of party in proceedings to challenge the decision of the court. On this occasion it is appropriate to thank the Senate and the senators for their commitment, their contribution to improving Polish law, listening to the voices of Polish citizens petitioning the Senate and their commitment to quickly execute the judgments of the Constitutional Tribunal. In conclusion, the public is looking forward to speeding up the work of the courts, and the project we are working on meets these expectations. By acting on behalf of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice, I declare that the club supports further work on the bill. At the same time, we believe that the rules still need to be refined in a number of places, which is why I am proposing two amendments on behalf of the Law and Justice Club. One of these aims is to enable the Supreme Court to refer the case to the Supreme Court for the sake of the good of the judiciary not only at the initiative of the court but also at the request of the prosecutor. The second amendment aims at adapting and harmonising the nomenclature for the Chief Prosecutor's Office as a procedural body in criminal proceedings. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

They regulate the so-called issue. a subsidiary indictment, i.e. initiation of proceedings in the event of double refusal by the prosecutor to report a suspected crime. The Ministry proposes to return to a solution that has already been in operation and which has simply not worked out, therefore it has been changed. The Ministry proposes re-introduction of the role of prosecutor over prosecutor refusing to initiate. They are clearly incompatible with the Directive of the European Parliament and of the Council of 9 March 2016 on the strengthening of certain aspects of the presumption of innocence and the right to be present at trial in criminal proceedings. Art. 117 § 3a c.p.k. the proposed wording provides that the absence of a party who has been duly informed of the procedural action, regardless of its cause, shall not preclude the conduct of that action if its defender or representative has presented himself. This does not, of course, apply to the action in which the party's participation is mandatory. The proposed amendment is in the first instance contrary to Article. Article 42 (2) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 2 constitutions. Nor does it meet the requirements of the fundamental principle of a fair process set out in the European Convention for the Protection of Human Rights and Fundamental Freedoms. The removal of the right to participate in the trial of the suspect, or later of the accused, who will justify his absence, in the sole fulfilment of the conditions of the defence's presence, constitutes a serious breach of the right of defence. In this case, the Constitutional Tribunal also ruled by a judgment of 28 April 2009, which the Ministry of Justice, contrary to what it claims in the explanatory memorandum of the bill, does not respect either. The proposed amendment is also flawed in practical terms, because it will not speed up proceedings due to the fact that such defendant's requests will be addressed, fully justified requests for re-trials, because they have been taken in his absence, which will naturally extend rather than shorten judicial proceedings. The proposal to introduce an article is even further to restrict the right of defence and undermine the idea of a fair trial. 378a Code of Criminal Procedure. This provision allows for the possibility of carrying out evidence proceedings at a hearing at which the defendant or defender has not been informed of its time limit, even if that person duly justifies his failure to appear. [[♪ The bell ♪]] The introduction of this provision will clearly expose Poland to cases in the European Court of Human Rights, cases which will be lost in advance. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u197	High Seym! Mr. Messengers! Gentlemen! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club Polish People's Party - Coalition Poland I have the honour to present my position on the draft law amending the Law on Lawyers and the Law on Legal Counsels. When leaning on this bill, you have to first answer the question: What was the designer's intention? This is the main reason for the particular life or health situation, which should not result in the automatic deletion of the solicitor's application and the solicitor's applicant from the list of applicants. In the current legal state, if the applicant does not take the exam as soon as the application is completed, the year after the application has been completed must be deleted from the list of applicants by the relevant local authority. Thus, he loses his right to replace a lawyer or counsel. As was rightly pointed out, the loss of this right may affect the professional situation of such an applicant. For example, after the loss of the right to replace the usefulness of such an applicant as an employee of the firm, it significantly decreases. The original wording of the draft law forgot to amend the article. Article 77 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 2 Law on Lawyer and Art. Article 35 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 2 Laws on Legal Counsels. Due to the comments made by the National Council of Legal Counsels, appropriate amendments were made. It is a pity that the current parliamentary majority, as well as the President-in-Office of Justice, so rarely listen to the voice of the Radical and Lawyers' Governments, which was particularly evident at the so-called. reform of the justice system. According to Art. Article 77 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 2 Law on Law on Lawyer and Art. Article 35 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 2 Law on legal counsels Lawyer and solicitor respectively can replace lawyer and legal counsel for a period of one year from the date of completion of the application indicated in the application certificate. The applicant's right to replace a lawyer and a lawyer respectively has been treated by the legislator independently of the applicant's status. Therefore, in order to ensure that the applicant does not lose this right and that his/her professional situation does not deteriorate, the provisions mentioned above need to be amended. As a result of the procedated amendment, the district attorney's council and the district council of the Chamber of Legal Counsels may, by a resolution at the request of the applicant submitted before the end of the year following the end of the application and on the basis of the documents presented, extend the period by 1 year in the event of certain circumstances preventing the entry into the bar examination as soon as possible after the end of the bar application. These circumstances include birth by the applicant of the child in the last year of the solicitor's application or during the period from the end of the child's examination to the date of completion of the bar examination or when the date of birth has been fixed at a date not later than 3 months from the date of completion of the bar examination, the applicant's occurrence of complications related to the course of pregnancy lasting at least 30 days during the last 6 months of the bar application or during the period from the end of the period until the end of the bar examination and the illness of the applicant causing incapacity to work for at least 90 days or requiring hospitalisation for at least 21 days during the last 6 months of the bar application or during the period from the end of the bar examination to the date of the end of the bar examination. As the Supreme Bar Council rightly points out, the birth of a child is a very important event for the mother's woman, but not only for the father's man. You can ask the question: What if, for example, The applicant's baby's mother will die in childbirth, and he's the only breadwinner in the family? What about a person who is a chronically ill child? A catalogue containing cases that allow for an extension of the period for entering the professional examination should be open and not closed, because life is always a step ahead of the law, never the other way around. Let him serve solicitors and counselors in a difficult situation of life. Thank you kindly. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u211	Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! I have the honour to present a report by the Committee on National Defence in print No 3601 on the government draft law amending the Act on Veterans of Action outside the State and some other laws, print No 3521. The Parliament held its 83rd meeting on 3 July. pursuant to Article. 39 (1) (a) 2 Rules of Procedure of the Sejm referred the above draft law to the Committee on National Defence for consideration. The Commission, after considering this draft law at its meeting on 4 July this year. requests that the High Sejm adopt the attached draft law. The draft law deals with the formulation of provisions enabling the system of assistance to be implemented more effectively to participants outside the national borders. It extends the scope of the matter and ensures greater consistency between the existing rules governing the powers of veterans and veterans affected. It is worth noting that the proposed amendment in some sense makes up for the backlog you have on veterans. It takes into account a number of demands raised by the veteran community in 2016 and eliminates doubts arising during the process of granting veteran or veteran status to the injured. The bill proposes, among others, increase the threshold of the allowance for the injured veteran, appreciating the commitment of the service to the risk of health and life during activities outside the country, granting an allowance of 5% dimension base for injured veteran who was found to be between 1% and 9% damage to health, thereby extending the entitlement to the allowance to all veterans affected. Until now, only people with 10% or higher health damage have been entitled to this supplement. Responding to the demands of the veterans' community and taking into account the possibilities to finance them, in order to ensure that the material situation of the veterans was improved, the principle that the higher the health loss, the greater the increase in the amount of the allowance for the injured veteran. In individual ranges, the amount of the supplement will be from 5% of the size base - precisely with a health risk of between 1% and 9%. - up to 130% of the size base if this loss is above 90%. In addition, a number of other benefits and allowances are proposed. Introduced, among others. The limitation of the Veteran's residence fee for the injured veteran has been extended to all veterans affected by reduced travel by public collective transport. The Act also introduces free use of sports halls, gyms or swimming pools in the resources of local self-government units. It is also expected that, along with the veteran injured in the tournaments, his next adult family member will be able to participate free of charge. The cost of such a stay will, of course, be borne by the state budget. Importantly, the bill was not controversial. During the discussion of this project, no more serious comments were made. I think this is very positive about the work of the current government and parliament. On behalf of the Committee on National Defence, I ask the High House to pass a bill. Thank you.

ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u238	Thank you very much, Member. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u239	Thank you very much. Mrs. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! I have the honour to present the position of the club Law and Justice on the presidential draft law on social services provided by the social services centre, print No. 3040. The aim of the legal solutions is to develop and integrate the social services system at local level. Social phenomena in recent years, the ageing of the population, the weakening of intergenerational ties in the family have led to a steady increase in demand for social services by public authorities. The legal framework to facilitate the integration and coordination of actions by local authorities, the introduction of new services, the improvement of their quality and quality of life of society and the better use of resources. The development of the social services system is considered to be an indication of the operation of the so-called "social services system." social investment policy. The creation of integrated local service systems fosters cross-sectoral cooperation and the exploitation of the potential of third sector organisations fosters the integration of territorial communities. The activities of the centres will facilitate access to social services for a wider population. Municipal authorities will be able to respond well and more comprehensively to the changing needs of local communities. In the systemic aspect, centres will constitute an institutional bridge between, on the one hand, social support organised according to the principle of selectivity and addressed to the most vulnerable people and families and, on the other hand, social support of a general nature with services offered to all concerned residents. Using the formula of the social service centre, support of a general and selective nature is mutually positioned in such a way that the support addressed to the most deprived becomes complementary to the universal programmes. The establishment of a social service centre will create optimal organisational and formal conditions for the development of social services as an important type of public service. At the same time, it will allow for the continuation of selective programmes aimed at vulnerable people and families under existing conditions using social services and their experience in social work. The concept of centres is consistent with national social policy. The integration of social services and the development of social area infrastructure, including in rural and outermost areas, is in line with the objectives of the Responsible Development Strategy. It is also consistent in substance with the 500+ programme, because the development of social services has a clear pro-family profile and will be built by local authorities to secure the needs of the family. The mission of the centres is actually to increase the availability of social services. The services will be addressed to all interested residents, not just the people and families most in need. The offer of centres will also provide a supply response to the growing demand for social services thanks to the 500+ programme. It should be added that such centres arise in our country bottom-up. In Warsaw, Lublin, Gdynia. They respond to the needs of local governments, have tangible effects, have a positive impact on the development of cooperation between local government and the third sector. The assumptions of this bill were widely presented, among others. by representatives of local government, experts, representatives of civil sector organisations, and the project itself was consulted with academics involved in social policy, members of the Social Assistance Council, representatives of local government, social workers and representatives of non-governmental organisations. Comments to the project were also made via e-mail. The project presented is undoubtedly a real answer to the challenges facing Poland, which Polish municipalities face in the field of social services. It introduces solutions to facilitate the organisation and efficient provision of coordinated social services for a growing group of people in need of such support from year to year. Taking all this into account, the Law and Justice Club welcomes the draft law presented and on the basis of the article. Article 45 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 1 Rules of Procedure of the Sejm of the Republic of Poland on behalf of the club Law and Justice I propose amendments to this project and ask for further proceedings over the draft law. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u265	The main objective of the draft act on the implementation of social services by the social services centre, which is the legislative initiative of the President of the Republic of Poland, is to develop and integrate the system of social services at local level. Social phenomena in recent years, in particular the ageing of the population and the weakening of intergenerational ties within the family, have resulted in a steady, dynamic increase in demand for social services organised by public authorities. Social expectations for the introduction of new types of services are also growing. The solutions contained in the draft law meet these expectations, creating a legal framework to facilitate the integration and coordination of local authorities, using a new organisational unit - a social service centre, various social services that meet the needs of the local community. The PSL Club will support this bill.

The aim of the project was to remedy the situation of the exhaustion of the guarantee amounts of mandatory OC insurance, i.e. civil liability, motor vehicle owners for damage caused by the use of these vehicles and farmers for holding an agricultural holding. This was the case for people who had accidents in the 1990s. The project, the original one, the Senate, introduced a new article that imposed an obligation on the Insurance Guarantee Fund to satisfy claims for personal injury. The Act provided that the injured party's claim for damages to the person by the Fund would cover the period from the date of exhaustion of the guarantee sum, but not earlier than the date of entry into force of the Act, and that the Act would enter into force 30 days after the date of publication. The Senate bill received a positive opinion from the government, from the Supreme Bar Council, the President of the Attorney General. This opinion was positive on the part of the Supreme Court, but with such strong attention that the law cannot act backwards and that this action has no constitutional justification. This Senate project was addressed to the Permanent Sub-Committee on Financial Institutions. He returned after almost 2 years under the amended title as a new project called the Act on the Specific Powers of the Victims in the event of exhaustion of the guarantee sum established under the provisions in force before 1 January 2004, print No 3574. ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u293	Mrs. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! On behalf of the Committee on Maritime Affairs and Inland Navigation, I have the honour to present a report to the committee on the Government's draft law amending the Law on ports and marinas and certain other laws. The first reading and examination of the draft law took place at the meeting of the Committee on Maritime Affairs and Inland Navigation on 16 July 2019. As part of the first reading, the Legislative Office of the Sejm adopted amendments of an editorial and legislative nature and a parliamentary amendment concerning the Law of 12 April 2018 on the Registration of Yachts and other vessels up to 24 m long. The aim of the project is to align the legal acts governing the organisation and operation of ports with the provisions of Regulation of the European Parliament and of the Council of 15 February 2017 establishing a framework for the provision of port services and common rules on the financial transparency of ports. This Regulation entered into force on 24 March 2019 and covers the scope of its application of TEN-T ports, i.e. ports in Szczecin, Świnoujście, Police, Gdańsk and of course Gdynia. According to the project, the managing body of the port will be responsible for the organisation of port services. The proposed law shall also specify the authority competent to deal with complaints from seaport users arising from the application of the Regulation by the managing body of the sea port or port service provider to which it will be the Director of the Maritime Authority. In addition, the managing body of the port will be entitled to lay down minimum conditions for the provision of port services and to limit the number of port service providers, if any. The possibility of exercising a majority is also introduced, excluding transhipment and handling of passengers, port services by the managing body of the port. The draft law also extends the competence of the maritime administration, the Minister responsible for maritime affairs, in the area of control over the marketing of real estate located at seaports. Notwithstanding this, amendments are made to the Law on Ports and Seaports in order to improve the operation of seaports. The amendments strengthen, in particular, the competence of port management to coordinate the use of port infrastructure, as well as the management of small municipal seaports for which the managing body will be able to become directly a municipality without the need to appoint a separate port management body. The Act also introduces a number of minor amendments to other legal acts, including: in the Real Estate Management Act, the Land Planning and Development Act, so-called. road specialization and law on maritime areas of the Republic of Poland and maritime administration. Particular attention should be paid to the amendments to the Public Roads Act allowing for the inclusion of roads to TEN-T ports in the category of national roads and ensuring that these roads are financed from budgetary resources, which is the responsibility of the General Directorate for National Roads and Motorways. At the first reading of the project, an amendment was tabled concerning the Law of 12 April 2018 on the Registration of Yachts and other vessels up to 24 m long. The main objective of the amendment is to waive the obligation to place units covered by the Control Marking Act on the hull. In addition, it is proposed to exclude from the registration of yachts and vessels used for amateur fishing, the length of which does not exceed 7,5 m or whose power of mechanical propulsion does not exceed 15 kW. A transitional period will also be extended for sailing yachts operated in inland waters which have not yet been subject to registration and for all yachts not yet subject to technical inspection. It is envisaged to postpone the entry into force of the provisions of the Act by 7 months. This period will allow the registration authorities to properly prepare for handling applications for registration. These changes will reduce the cost of boat registration and thus contribute to the development of sailing and activities operating in smaller ports and marinas. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! As rapporteur on behalf of the Committee on Maritime Affairs and Inland Navigation, I recommend the adoption of this draft law amending the Law on ports and marinas and certain other laws. Thank you.

ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u295	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Law and Justice Club, I have the honour to present a position towards the Government's draft law amending the Law on Ports and Seaports and some other laws, print No 3582. The main objective of the project submitted by the government is to align the legislation governing the organisation and operation of ports with the provisions of the European Parliament Regulation of 15 February 2017 establishing a framework for the provision of port services and common rules on the financial transparency of ports which entered into force on 24 March 2019. The port regulation is applied explicitly, but it is necessary to adapt the existing national rules to the framework set out in its rules. The provisions of the port regulation apply to ports located in the TEN-T network, i.e. in the case of Poland to ports: Gdańsk, Gdynia, Szczecin, Świnoujście and Police. In order to bring the provisions of the Ports and Seaports Act into line with the provisions of the Regulation, amendments are introduced, inter alia. to enable the port manager to introduce minimum conditions for the provision of port services and to reduce the number of port service providers, if any. In addition, the revised rules allow port services to be carried out independently by the managing body of the port, but subject to the application of the restrictions laid down in the port regulation and excluding transhipment and handling of passengers. The provisions of the Act also define the Director of the Maritime Authority as the authority competent to deal with complaints arising from incorrect application of the provisions of the Regulation. The maximum limits for port charges will also be lifted. Notwithstanding the adaptation of the provisions of the Act to EU legislation, the proposed Act strengthens the competence of the Minister responsible for maritime affairs in the field of real estate trading located in the port area in order to maintain port functions on these properties. It clarifies the management of municipal seaports without a separate management body. The draft law also amends a number of laws aimed at improving the operation of seaports. Among the most important are the amendment of the Public Road Act introducing the possibility for GDDKiA to finance access routes to the TEN-T core network ports by including them in the category of national roads providing access to seaports. Changes are also made to the Real Estate Management Act, which includes port investments in public-purpose investments, amendments to the Land Planning and Land Planning Act which make it compulsory to agree with the Port Management Board on local land-use plans and decisions on construction conditions, amendments to the Law on specific rules for preparing the implementation of public roads investments by consulting the Port Management Board of the application for a decision to authorise road investments and amendments to the Law on Maritime Areas of the Republic of Poland and the Maritime Administration extending periods of permits for the construction of artificial islands, constructions and equipment. This issue is of fundamental importance for the development of Polish wind energy. A parliamentary amendment was also tabled, and I submitted it personally, concerning the Act of 12 April 2018 on registration of yachts and other vessels up to 24 m long. It aims primarily to abandon the introduction of a check mark for yachts and other vessels up to 24 m long. The amendments covered by the amendment include signals from shipowners and owners of yachts and meet social expectations. They will not adversely affect the safety of vessels. They were welcomed by the Ministry of Maritime Affairs and Inland Shipping. Importantly, the proposed amendment will contribute by facilitating sailing to the development of small ports and seaports, thus contributing to the assumption of a draft law presented by the government. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, I ask the High House to accept this proposal in the wording proposed by the Committee on Maritime Affairs and Inland Navigation and to take account of the Parliamentary amendment tabled. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u307	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! On behalf of the Poselski Club Kukiz'15, I have the honour to present a position regarding the Act amending the Law on Ports and Seaports and some other laws, print No. 3582. The draft concerns the adaptation of national provisions to Regulation (EU) 2017/352 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 15 February 2017 establishing a framework for the provision of port services and common rules on the financial transparency of ports. In view of the need to bring national legislation into line with the abovementioned provisions. a regulation is necessary to intervene at the level of the Act, which will concern ports in Gdynia, Gdańsk, Szczecin - Świnoujście and Police. The proposed regulation shall specify the authority competent to deal with complaints from port users by the managing body of the port or port service provider to which the Director of the Maritime Office will be. In addition, in order to bring national provisions into line with Regulation (EU) 2017/352 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 15 February 2017, the draft provides for amendments to the following laws: 1) the maritime areas of the Republic of Poland and the maritime administration - the changes resulting from the amendment of the Law on ports and marinas, in particular as regards the competence of the directors of maritime offices; 2) planning and spatial planning; 3) on public roads; 4) with specific rules for the preparation and implementation of public road investments; 5. on real estate management; 6. port reception facilities and cargo residues from ships; 7. electromobility and alternative fuels; 8. the registration of yachts and other vessels; 9) about public documents. The introduction of EU regulation will result in: - ensuring that complaints are dealt with by the Maritime Authority in Gdynia and the Maritime Authority in Szczecin. Changes in small port management shit; - the need to adapt the marina organisation to the rules laid down in the proposed law, in particular in the area of maritime real estate management; - to refrain from setting a maximum limit on port charges at the level of the Act, which will result in the possibility of setting them at a higher level than at present; - extending the scope of legal activities requiring the Minister's approval in the field of real estate management in ports, resulting in an increase in the burden of the tasks of the Minister of Office; - the possibility to lodge a complaint with the competent Director of the Maritime Authority concerning the infringement of Regulation No 2017/352. In our view, the introduction of this bill will improve the functioning of Polish ports and marinas, including services provided in this field, which will strengthen their position in the international market, especially in the Baltic Sea basin. Therefore, the Kukiz Parliamentary Club15 supports this regulation.

This is the effect of nearly 4 years of the rule of law and justice in the field of energy. The symbol of this diagnosis is the construction of a coal power plant in Ostrołęka, which will be the most modern energy museum producing the most expensive electricity in the European Union. It is stated in the explanatory memorandum that in Polish conditions the impact of emission rights prices on the price of electricity is extremely high. The Polish market is isolated from neighbouring markets and therefore there is no competitive pressure on energy prices. The high impact of rights prices on wholesale prices is the result of the electricity generation structure in Poland. This structure is based on coal primacy and only 13.7% energy comes from RES. This means a steady increase in electricity costs due to the need to pay for too much CO2 and greenhouse gas emissions. In this way, the government critically evaluates its actions for the competitiveness of Polish energy, as it will have to buy more and more expensive powers, while e.g. in Germany, due to the increasing share of renewable energy, prices will decrease. Electricity in Poland will be more expensive and energy in RES countries will be cheaper. Conclusion: in the near future, the key task will be to help energy-intensive companies by introducing a law to compensate for the rise in the price of allowances, but in addition - and this is no longer mentioned in the explanatory memorandum of the Act - it is necessary to change the energy strategy in such a way that we produce cheap energy, as the Civic Coalition in the six Schetins says, and gradually move away from coal in the energy industry and significantly increase the share of RES in the energy mix. The draft law was drawn up at the last moment of the ETS Directive in its form until 2020 and was created due to a drastic increase in the price of allowances: from EUR 5 to EUR 26. The provisions of the Act provide for indirect cost compensation to be granted in such a way that final customers bear up to 44% of the costs allowances prices, although electricity will still be lower in Germany in this case. The financial resources for the payment of compensation for the calendar year concerned shall amount to not more than 25% revenues from the sale of emission allowances by auction. The Act establishes the Mid-Emission Cost Compensation Fund. With the rise in emission allowances prices, the compensation value will increase in 2020 and 2021, a total of PLN 890 million is foreseen. But the question is, how long can you base? on public aid in this formula and whether the European Union will accept this solution after 2020. There is also a question about other price-setting components in energy, which are often subject to political action. ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u335	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! I have the great honour to submit a report to the Committee on Infrastructure on the Government's draft Act on Accessibility to Persons with Special Needs, Prints 3579 and 3644. I would like to focus in this report on recalling some of the key elements governing accessibility for people with special needs, because this project is a completely new project, it is a system project, it is a comprehensive project, for the first time a project defining and needs, and solutions for the design and implementation of many projects serving them. I would like to point out that the solutions provided for in the bill are primarily intended to improve the living conditions of citizens with particular needs, who are exposed to marginalisation, discrimination, among others. due to disability or reduced level of fitness due to age or disease. The project sets out measures to ensure architectural accessibility, digital accessibility and information and communication accessibility for people with special needs throughout the public sphere and where public funds are being used. This project is an important step towards building a new image of public entities. The law is what I would also like to stress - and to pay my respects here to the Ministry of Investment and Development - a key element of the Government's Accessibility Programme+, which the Council of Ministers adopted in July 2018, and the solutions provided for in the draft implement the provisions set out in the United Nations Convention on the Rights of Persons with Disabilities which oblige such persons on an equal footing with other citizens to have independent access to public services. The public sector will be obliged, in accordance with a project subject to the procedure in Parliament, to ensure at least minimum availability in this respect, and the public body will ensure such availability by applying universal design of new solutions or reasonable improvements to the removal of existing barriers. Minimum requirements in this respect will cover architectural, digital and information and communication accessibility. In an individual case, a public entity, if it is unable, in particular for technical or legal reasons, to provide such availability, will have to provide alternative access, in particular by providing a person with specific needs for support of another person, e.g. I'm an assistant. And here I would like to cite an example that illustrates the work on this project. This project was probably consulted with more than 67 social organisations to which the government addressed it. There were more than 700 comments from stakeholders to whom the project was addressed in the course of its work. For example, one of the representatives of the cooperating associations, subject to the procession, participated in this together with a dog guide named Perper. So that is exactly what we have seen as Members who have so far been involved in the process of this document. The Minister for Regional Development will coordinate the work on accessibility and the Minister responsible for regional development will be set up and the Accessibility Council will function as a very important consultative body. According to the project, the Accessibility Fund will be set up. This fund will be a source of funding for accessibility measures for residents. It will be set up on the basis of the Act and will aim at providing financial support for the adaptation of public buildings and multi-family housing to the needs of people with special needs. The proposal also provides, which I would like to highlight very much, sanctions for lack of accessibility, and I would like to point out that this is a very expectation by the environment of people with disabilities, because the inefficiencies of existing solutions and regulations are largely due today to the lack of consequences of non-compliance. The Act is to enter into force 14 days after the date of its publication in the Official Journal of the Laws, but meeting the expectations of the public sector and taking into account the need to provide an adequate time to adapt to the changes, we have agreed that some of the regulations will apply in other time limits. This concerns, in particular, the need to appoint an accessibility coordinator, which will enter into force from September 2020. - or the entry into force of laws allowing complaints about lack of availability - here we have a vacathio legis of 24 months - or the possibility of certification of private entities - for this we have 18 months after the entry into force of the Act. In conclusion, I would like to present here, your marshal, High Izbo, a few important conclusions, I think, as a result of work and government, and the Sejm, which is proceeding on this very important not to say: historical for the environment of people with particular needs of the project. In Poland 12% Poles, i.e. 5 million citizens, are people to some extent with some kind of disability. Today we are one of the fastest ageing societies on the old continent. By 2020, the number of senior citizens will already reach 10 million, or 1/4 of the population. The Act on Accessibility for people with special needs is the first horizontal systemic approach to combating discrimination, to the need to open up to the needs of older people and people with disabilities. This law is an expression of a solidarity policy of the government that rejects social exclusion or economic inequality. This was emphasised by the current Deputy Minister of Investment and Development at the meeting of the Committee on Infrastructure on several occasions in the discussion. I would also like to make it very clear that this law is the key to opening the door to public institutions to people with disabilities, seniors, parents with children, generally all who have special needs related to their health. And thanks to this law, the public sector, offices, schools, universities, health care institutions will have to ensure architectural accessibility or availability in communication. This law introduces not only obligations but also numerous accessibility instruments, establishes accessibility coordinators in public authorities, creates this Accessibility Board as an expert advisory body, or regulates accessibility certification for private entities. For the first time on such a scale, the Act creates sources of funding for these tasks. It creates the Accessibility Fund mentioned earlier by me - the first ever financial solution dedicated to removing barriers on such a scale. This law opens the world, in quotes, as it is said in the common language, to the prisoners of the fourth floor, to tens of thousands of people who are trapped in their homes because of the lack of an elevator. or no driveway. This law opens up the possibility of financing such investments. This law is about meeting the needs and meeting the expectations of a large part of society on an unknown scale. Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, I think that each of us, with a public mandate, often in his parliamentary work, in the parliamentary office, during meetings in the country or locally, in his city, met with people who needed it to give them access to public services, offices and many other places. Today, these people are often very difficult, to a lesser extent, to access these places. I think that it is often difficult for a young, healthy person to understand how important accessibility is. Imagine that we would call a conference, a meeting, and you wouldn't be able to enter the building because there's no open door, no open space, you have to climb. Today someone can say: What is this availability? It doesn't concern me. But I can assure you that in a few, a dozen or several decades, this will apply to each of us because we're all getting older. Mrs. Marshal! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, I am very pleased to announce that the Act on Accessibility to people with special needs has met with a very positive response from many ladies and gentlemen of Members, and, above all, it has received extremely warm and very good acceptance from the social representation present in the work on the procedated act. I think that this law in this form is above all a concrete expression of the hope that the design process, the process of changes in public space will proceed in a better way, in a perfect way, in identifying the needs of people with disabilities, to which access to such a public space should be ensured. There is also a new model for designing architectural solutions - after the entry into force of this regulation. This is above all, as I said, meeting needs, fulfilling the hopes and expectations of a large part of society on an unprecedented scale. In conclusion, as the rapporteur of the committee, I once again express my great thanks to the leadership of the Ministry of Investment and Development, which led this project in the Sejm, and to the whole government for adopting this initiative so important for hundreds of thousands of people in our country. ParlaMint-PL_2019-07-17-sejm-84-1.u336	Thank you very much, sir.
